| Rui | I see... You're worried that you could be doing a better job of interacting with Tsukasa and Asahi as they play Bartlett... | talk |
| Asahi | Really? I thought you were doing just fine. | talk |
| Rui | But you believe you could be doing better... Right, Nene? | talk |
| Nene | Y-Yeah, I think so... | talk |
| Emu | Oh! Oh! I wanna learn how to interact with them better too! Lots better! | talk |
| Asahi | Thank you, Emu.<br>In that case, well... | talk |
| Asahi | This may not sound all that profound, but doesn't that have to do with really seeing how the person opposite to you is acting? | talk |
| Emu | Really seeing how they're acting...? | talk |
| Nene | I thought I did... | talk |
| Rui | Yes, I agree that you've both established a good foundation. However, there does appear to be one difficult bit. | talk |
| Rui | Do you concur, Asahi? | talk |
| Asahi | Yes. For example, the scene we just practiced... | talk |
| Asahi | Remember how you spoke with your back turned to Bartlett, Nene? You were treating him like someone who deserves that little respect, no? | talk |
| Nene | Oh, you're right...<br>I wasn't looking at Tsukasa's face that time. | talk |
| Rui | She's doing a good job of showing how Sergeant Willis feels about Bartlett, so I'm not sure we can do much to change that part. | talk |
| Asahi | I noticed something when Tsukasa and I played that scene just now. When I say, “Please excuse my insolence,” I never looked towards Nene. | talk |
| Asahi | But when Tsukasa played Bartlett, he tried to make eye contact with you. | talk |
| Nene | Huh...? | talk |
| Rui | I believe that is Tsukasa's way of presenting a small aspect of Bartlett's humanity. | talk |
| Asahi | I liked what he did there too. It felt like he was making a real effort to make a human connection. | talk |
| Nene | Really?<br>I had no idea... | talk |
| Emu | Yeah, there are times when I can't tell what other people are doing because I have my back turned to them... | talk |

| Asahi | So, when you're not sure, it's good to ask others who watch you practice about particular scenes. | talk |
| Nene | Oh, now I understand... | talk |
| Rui | Hehe. We've been making an effort to dissect our performances up until now, but it's nice to be taking such a deep dive like this. | talk |
| Emu | Yeah! I'm gonna try asking more people from now on! | talk |
| Nene | Me too. | talk |
| Nene | (Asking is easy, and even though it's not like I did until now... Being more intentional about it is gonna make a huge difference moving forward.) | talk |
